First run of the tests for ticket #22, before the feature exists, on Pixel_10
(emulator-5554), with no code for the ticket written yet.

Result: 6 of the 7 tests fail because the feature is missing, and 1 passes.
There are no SETUP failures.

- The four tests that check the kept photo fail because it is 720x1280, the
  camera's whole picture, not a square cut to the dashed frame. The dashed frame
  was found on the camera screen in every one of them, in both camera views.
- The two crop-failure tests reached the camera's picture and spoiled it, as
  planned. The app then opened the photo screen with the broken picture
  ("Exception: Invalid image data") instead of staying on the camera screen
  with the note.
- "Given the person taps the shutter, when they press back before the temporary
  screen opens, then home shows and no photo is kept" passes. The app already
  drops a photo that arrives after the person has left. It stays as a guard,
  because the crop adds a step (see docs/tests/22.md).

The test runner's colour codes were removed from the output below. Nothing else
was changed.

Before this run, the frame comparison's limit in helpers/dashed_frame.dart was
measured against photos the app takes today, in both camera views, with a
temporary script that wrote no files and wasn't kept. That measured the tests'
tool. It was not a run of the tests and not a stand-in version of the feature.
